+namespace {
+
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_SEED = 256;
+
+// CUDA errors are fatal.  CUDA is deliberately not optional for this
+// benchmark: silently running a CPU implementation would make its reported
+// performance meaningless.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkKernel(const char* operation) {
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), operation);
+}
+
+// The matrix is laid out as distance[member * N + candidate].  Keeping it
+// resident avoids recalculating the same pairwise distance for every seed and
+// every QT growth iteration.  The 2-D launch gives coalesced writes along the
+// candidate dimension, which is also the access pattern used by score updates.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count || member >= point_count) return;
+
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate] =
+        sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Scores use two negative sentinels.  -1 means that the point is already in
+// this seed's candidate cluster, while -2 means that it was assigned by a
+// previously selected global cluster.  Non-negative values are the current
+// maximum distance from the candidate to this candidate cluster.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateState(const double* distances,
+                                          const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                          double* scores,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (clustered[candidate] != 0) {
+        scores[offset] = -2.0;
+    } else if (seed == candidate) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+    } else {
+        // This is distance(points[candidate], points[seed]), matching the
+        // order used by the original candidate-to-member calculation.
+        scores[offset] = distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeSeedState(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                    unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                    const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const bool is_active = clustered[seed] == 0;
+    active[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+    member_counts[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool isBetter(const double score, const int candidate,
+                                          const double best_score,
+                                          const int best_candidate) {
+    return score < best_score ||
+           (score == best_score && candidate < best_candidate);
+}
+
+// One block owns one seed.  Each thread scans a strided portion of all
+// candidates and the block reduction retains the lowest candidate index on an
+// exact score tie, just like the original ascending CPU loop.
+__global__ void selectClosestCandidates(const double* scores,
+                                        const unsigned char* active,
+                                        int* best_candidates,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+    double best_score = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    if (active[seed] != 0) {
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            const double score = scores[row + candidate];
+            if (score >= 0.0 && score < threshold &&
+                isBetter(score, candidate, best_score, best_candidate)) {
+                best_score = score;
+                best_candidate = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double shared_scores[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    __shared__ int shared_candidates[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = best_score;
+    shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            isBetter(shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_scores[threadIdx.x],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x])) {
+            shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        best_candidates[seed] = shared_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                    ? -1
+                                    : shared_candidates[0];
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the chosen point to every seed's state in parallel.  Once a point is
+// selected, the score update is just one lookup into the resident distance
+// matrix for each still-eligible candidate.
+__global__ void updateCandidateScores(const double* distances,
+                                      const int* best_candidates,
+                                      double* scores, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const int best = best_candidates[seed];
+    if (best < 0) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (candidate == best) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double old_score = scores[offset];
+    if (old_score >= 0.0) {
+        const double new_distance =
+            distances[static_cast<size_t>(best) * point_count + candidate];
+        scores[offset] = old_score > new_distance ? old_score : new_distance;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void commitCandidates(const int* best_candidates,
+                                  unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                  int* added_count, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    if (active[seed] == 0) return;
+
+    if (best_candidates[seed] < 0) {
+        active[seed] = 0;
+    } else {
+        ++member_counts[seed];
+        atomicAdd(added_count, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

 };
 
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_SEED = 256;
+
+// CUDA errors are fatal.  CUDA is deliberately not optional for this
+// benchmark: silently running a CPU implementation would make its reported
+// performance meaningless.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkKernel(const char* operation) {
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), operation);
+}
+
+// The matrix is laid out as distance[member * N + candidate].  Keeping it
+// resident avoids recalculating the same pairwise distance for every seed and
+// every QT growth iteration.  The 2-D launch gives coalesced writes along the
+// candidate dimension, which is also the access pattern used by score updates.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count || member >= point_count) return;
+
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate] =
+        sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Scores use two negative sentinels.  -1 means that the point is already in
+// this seed's candidate cluster, while -2 means that it was assigned by a
+// previously selected global cluster.  Non-negative values are the current
+// maximum distance from the candidate to this candidate cluster.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateState(const double* distances,
+                                          const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                          double* scores,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (clustered[candidate] != 0) {
+        scores[offset] = -2.0;
+    } else if (seed == candidate) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+    } else {
+        // This is distance(points[candidate], points[seed]), matching the
+        // order used by the original candidate-to-member calculation.
+        scores[offset] = distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeSeedState(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                    unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                    const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const bool is_active = clustered[seed] == 0;
+    active[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+    member_counts[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool isBetter(const double score, const int candidate,
+                                          const double best_score,
+                                          const int best_candidate) {
+    return score < best_score ||
+           (score == best_score && candidate < best_candidate);
+}
+
+// One block owns one seed.  Each thread scans a strided portion of all
+// candidates and the block reduction retains the lowest candidate index on an
+// exact score tie, just like the original ascending CPU loop.
+__global__ void selectClosestCandidates(const double* scores,
+                                        const unsigned char* active,
+                                        int* best_candidates,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+    double best_score = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    if (active[seed] != 0) {
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            const double score = scores[row + candidate];
+            if (score >= 0.0 && score < threshold &&
+                isBetter(score, candidate, best_score, best_candidate)) {
+                best_score = score;
+                best_candidate = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double shared_scores[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    __shared__ int shared_candidates[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = best_score;
+    shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            isBetter(shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_scores[threadIdx.x],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x])) {
+            shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        best_candidates[seed] = shared_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                    ? -1
+                                    : shared_candidates[0];
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the chosen point to every seed's state in parallel.  Once a point is
+// selected, the score update is just one lookup into the resident distance
+// matrix for each still-eligible candidate.
+__global__ void updateCandidateScores(const double* distances,
+                                      const int* best_candidates,
+                                      double* scores, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const int best = best_candidates[seed];
+    if (best < 0) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (candidate == best) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double old_score = scores[offset];
+    if (old_score >= 0.0) {
+        const double new_distance =
+            distances[static_cast<size_t>(best) * point_count + candidate];
+        scores[offset] = old_score > new_distance ? old_score : new_distance;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void commitCandidates(const int* best_candidates,
+                                  unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                  int* added_count, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    if (active[seed] == 0) return;
+
+    if (best_candidates[seed] < 0) {
+        active[seed] = 0;
+    } else {
+        ++member_counts[seed];
+        atomicAdd(added_count, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

     return clusters;

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA version
     2	// 
     3	// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
     4	// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
     5	// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
     6	
     7	#include <algorithm>
     8	#include <chrono>
     9	#include <cmath>
    10	#include <cfloat>
    11	#include <cstdint>
    12	#include <cstdio>
    13	#include <cstdlib>
    14	#include <cstring>
    15	#include <limits>
    16	#include <vector>
    17	
    18	#include <cuda_runtime.h>
    19	
    20	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    21	
    22	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    23	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    24	
    25	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    26	struct Point {
    27	    double x, y;
    28	};
    29	
    30	// Structure to represent a cluster
    31	struct Cluster {
    32	    std::vector<int> members;
    33	    int seed_point;
    34	};
    35	
    36	namespace {
    37	
    38	constexpr int THREADS_PER_SEED = 256;
    39	
    40	// CUDA errors are fatal.  CUDA is deliberately not optional for this
    41	// benchmark: silently running a CPU implementation would make its reported
    42	// performance meaningless.
    43	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
    44	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
    45	        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
    46	                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
    47	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    48	    }
    49	}
    50	
    51	void checkKernel(const char* operation) {
    52	    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), operation);
    53	}
    54	
    55	// The matrix is laid out as distance[member * N + candidate].  Keeping it
    56	// resident avoids recalculating the same pairwise distance for every seed and
    57	// every QT growth iteration.  The 2-D launch gives coalesced writes along the
    58	// candidate dimension, which is also the access pattern used by score updates.
    59	__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances,
    60	                                     const int point_count) {
    61	    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    62	    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    63	    if (candidate >= point_count || member >= point_count) return;
    64	
    65	    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
    66	    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
    67	    distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate] =
    68	        sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    69	}
    70	
    71	// Scores use two negative sentinels.  -1 means that the point is already in
    72	// this seed's candidate cluster, while -2 means that it was assigned by a
    73	// previously selected global cluster.  Non-negative values are the current
    74	// maximum distance from the candidate to this candidate cluster.
    75	__global__ void initializeCandidateState(const double* distances,
    76	                                          const unsigned char* clustered,
    77	                                          double* scores,
    78	                                          const int point_count) {
    79	    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    80	    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    81	    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
    82	
    83	    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
    84	    if (clustered[candidate] != 0) {
    85	        scores[offset] = -2.0;
    86	    } else if (seed == candidate) {
    87	        scores[offset] = -1.0;
    88	    } else {
    89	        // This is distance(points[candidate], points[seed]), matching the
    90	        // order used by the original candidate-to-member calculation.
    91	        scores[offset] = distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
    92	    }
    93	}
    94	
    95	__global__ void initializeSeedState(const unsigned char* clustered,
    96	                                    unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
    97	                                    const int point_count) {
    98	    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    99	    if (seed >= point_count) return;
   100	
   101	    const bool is_active = clustered[seed] == 0;
   102	    active[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
   103	    member_counts[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
   104	}
   105	
   106	__device__ __forceinline__ bool isBetter(const double score, const int candidate,
   107	                                          const double best_score,
   108	                                          const int best_candidate) {
   109	    return score < best_score ||
   110	           (score == best_score && candidate < best_candidate);
   111	}
   112	
   113	// One block owns one seed.  Each thread scans a strided portion of all
   114	// candidates and the block reduction retains the lowest candidate index on an
   115	// exact score tie, just like the original ascending CPU loop.
   116	__global__ void selectClosestCandidates(const double* scores,
   117	                                        const unsigned char* active,
   118	                                        int* best_candidates,
   119	                                        const double threshold,
   120	                                        const int point_count) {
   121	    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
   122	    if (seed >= point_count) return;
   123	
   124	    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
   125	    double best_score = DBL_MAX;
   126	    int best_candidate = point_count;
   127	
   128	    if (active[seed] != 0) {
   129	        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
   130	             candidate += blockDim.x) {
   131	            const double score = scores[row + candidate];
   132	            if (score >= 0.0 && score < threshold &&
   133	                isBetter(score, candidate, best_score, best_candidate)) {
   134	                best_score = score;
   135	                best_candidate = candidate;
   136	            }
   137	        }
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    __shared__ double shared_scores[THREADS_PER_SEED];
   141	    __shared__ int shared_candidates[THREADS_PER_SEED];
   142	    shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = best_score;
   143	    shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = best_candidate;
   144	    __syncthreads();
   145	
   146	    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
   147	        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
   148	            isBetter(shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride],
   149	                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride],
   150	                     shared_scores[threadIdx.x],
   151	                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x])) {
   152	            shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride];
   153	            shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride];
   154	        }
   155	        __syncthreads();
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
   159	        best_candidates[seed] = shared_candidates[0] == point_count
   160	                                    ? -1
   161	                                    : shared_candidates[0];
   162	    }
   163	}
   164	
   165	// Add the chosen point to every seed's state in parallel.  Once a point is
   166	// selected, the score update is just one lookup into the resident distance
   167	// matrix for each still-eligible candidate.
   168	__global__ void updateCandidateScores(const double* distances,
   169	                                      const int* best_candidates,
   170	                                      double* scores, const int point_count) {
   171	    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
   172	    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   173	    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
   174	
   175	    const int best = best_candidates[seed];
   176	    if (best < 0) return;
   177	
   178	    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
   179	    if (candidate == best) {
   180	        scores[offset] = -1.0;
   181	        return;
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    const double old_score = scores[offset];
   185	    if (old_score >= 0.0) {
   186	        const double new_distance =
   187	            distances[static_cast<size_t>(best) * point_count + candidate];
   188	        scores[offset] = old_score > new_distance ? old_score : new_distance;
   189	    }
   190	}
   191	
   192	__global__ void commitCandidates(const int* best_candidates,
   193	                                  unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
   194	                                  int* added_count, const int point_count) {
   195	    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   196	    if (seed >= point_count) return;
   197	
   198	    if (active[seed] == 0) return;
   199	
   200	    if (best_candidates[seed] < 0) {
   201	        active[seed] = 0;
   202	    } else {
   203	        ++member_counts[seed];
   204	        atomicAdd(added_count, 1);
   205	    }
   206	}
   207	
   208	} // namespace
   209	
   210	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
   211	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
   212	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
   213	    
   214	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
   215	    int count = 0;
   216	    
   217	    while (count < N) {
   218	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
   219	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
   220	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
   221	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
   222	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
   223	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
   224	        
   225	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
   226	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
   227	            group_cnt = N - count;
   228	        }
   229	        
   230	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
   231	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
   232	            const double r = frand() * R;
   233	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
   234	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
   235	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
   236	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
   237	            
   238	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
   239	                continue;
   240	            }
   241	            
   242	            points[count] = {x, y};
   243	            count++;
   244	            group_cnt--;
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	}
   248	
   249	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
   250	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
   251	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
   252	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
   253	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   254	}
   255	
   256	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   257	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   258	                                  const double threshold) {
   259	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   260	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
   261	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   262	
   263	    if (N == 0) return clusters;
   264	
   265	    int device_count = 0;
   266	    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
   267	    if (device_count == 0) {
   268	        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
   269	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   270	    }
   271	    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(0), "cudaSetDevice");
   272	
   273	    const size_t matrix_elements = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N;
   274	    const size_t matrix_bytes = matrix_elements * sizeof(double);
   275	
   276	    Point* device_points = nullptr;
   277	    double* device_distances = nullptr;
   278	    double* device_scores = nullptr;
   279	    unsigned char* device_clustered = nullptr;
   280	    unsigned char* device_active = nullptr;
   281	    int* device_best_candidates = nullptr;
   282	    int* device_member_counts = nullptr;
   283	    int* device_added_count = nullptr;
   284	
   285	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_points, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)),
   286	              "cudaMalloc(points)");
   287	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_distances, matrix_bytes),
   288	              "cudaMalloc(distances)");
   289	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_scores, matrix_bytes), "cudaMalloc(scores)");
   290	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_clustered, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
   291	              "cudaMalloc(clustered)");
   292	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_active, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
   293	              "cudaMalloc(active)");
   294	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_best_candidates, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)),
   295	              "cudaMalloc(best candidates)");
   296	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_member_counts, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)),
   297	              "cudaMalloc(member counts)");
   298	    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&device_added_count, sizeof(int)),
   299	              "cudaMalloc(added count)");
   300	
   301	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(device_points, points.data(),
   302	                         static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),
   303	                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   304	              "cudaMemcpy(points)");
   305	    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(device_clustered, clustered.data(),
   306	                         static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(unsigned char),
   307	                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   308	              "cudaMemcpy(clustered)");
   309	
   310	    constexpr int TILE_X = 32;
   311	    constexpr int TILE_Y = 8;
   312	    const dim3 matrix_block(TILE_X, TILE_Y);
   313	    const dim3 matrix_grid((N + TILE_X - 1) / TILE_X,
   314	                           (N + TILE_Y - 1) / TILE_Y);
   315	    buildDistanceMatrix<<<matrix_grid, matrix_block>>>(
   316	        device_points, device_distances, N);
   317	    checkKernel("buildDistanceMatrix");
   318	
   319	    const dim3 state_block(TILE_X, TILE_Y);
   320	    const dim3 state_grid((N + TILE_X - 1) / TILE_X,
   321	                          (N + TILE_Y - 1) / TILE_Y);
   322	    const int seed_grid = (N + THREADS_PER_SEED - 1) / THREADS_PER_SEED;
   323	    const dim3 seed_block(THREADS_PER_SEED);
   324	
   325	    std::vector<int> member_counts(N, 0);
   326	    std::vector<double> best_scores(N);
   327	    int remaining = N;
   328	
   329	    while (remaining > 0) {
   330	        initializeCandidateState<<<state_grid, state_block>>>(
   331	            device_distances, device_clustered, device_scores, N);
   332	        checkKernel("initializeCandidateState");
   333	        initializeSeedState<<<seed_grid, seed_block>>>(
   334	            device_clustered, device_active, device_member_counts, N);
   335	        checkKernel("initializeSeedState");
   336	
   337	        int added_count = 0;
   338	        do {
   339	            selectClosestCandidates<<<N, THREADS_PER_SEED>>>(
   340	                device_scores, device_active, device_best_candidates,
   341	                threshold, N);
   342	            checkKernel("selectClosestCandidates");
   343	            updateCandidateScores<<<state_grid, state_block>>>(
   344	                device_distances, device_best_candidates, device_scores, N);
   345	            checkKernel("updateCandidateScores");
   346	            checkCuda(cudaMemset(device_added_count, 0, sizeof(int)),
   347	                      "cudaMemset(added count)");
   348	            commitCandidates<<<seed_grid, seed_block>>>(
   349	                device_best_candidates, device_active, device_member_counts,
   350	                device_added_count, N);
   351	            checkKernel("commitCandidates");
   352	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(&added_count, device_added_count, sizeof(int),
   353	                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   354	                      "cudaMemcpy(added count)");
   355	        } while (added_count != 0);
   356	
   357	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(member_counts.data(), device_member_counts,
   358	                             static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int),
   359	                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   360	                  "cudaMemcpy(member counts)");
   361	
   362	        // This is the one intentionally sequential part of QT: the first
   363	        // seed with the largest candidate cardinality wins the round.
   364	        int max_cardinality = -1;
   365	        int best_seed = -1;
   366	        for (int seed = 0; seed < N; ++seed) {
   367	            if (clustered[seed] == 0 && member_counts[seed] > max_cardinality) {
   368	                max_cardinality = member_counts[seed];
   369	                best_seed = seed;
   370	            }
   371	        }
   372	
   373	        if (best_seed < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) break;
   374	
   375	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(best_scores.data(),
   376	                             device_scores + static_cast<size_t>(best_seed) * N,
   377	                             static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(double),
   378	                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   379	                  "cudaMemcpy(best cluster state)");
   380	
   381	        Cluster cluster;
   382	        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
   383	        cluster.members.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(max_cardinality));
   384	        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {
   385	            if (best_scores[candidate] == -1.0) {
   386	                cluster.members.push_back(candidate);
   387	            }
   388	        }
   389	
   390	        // A selected point is encoded exactly as -1.  Keeping this check
   391	        // protects the host-side bookkeeping if a future kernel changes the
   392	        // state representation.
   393	        if (static_cast<int>(cluster.members.size()) != max_cardinality) {
   394	            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: candidate cardinality mismatch\n");
   395	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   396	        }
   397	
   398	        clusters.push_back(cluster);
   399	        for (const int member : cluster.members) {
   400	            clustered[member] = 1;
   401	            --remaining;
   402	        }
   403	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(device_clustered, clustered.data(),
   404	                             static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(unsigned char),
   405	                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   406	                  "cudaMemcpy(clustered update)");
   407	    }
   408	
   409	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_added_count), "cudaFree(added count)");
   410	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_member_counts), "cudaFree(member counts)");
   411	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_best_candidates), "cudaFree(best candidates)");
   412	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_active), "cudaFree(active)");
   413	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_clustered), "cudaFree(clustered)");
   414	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_scores), "cudaFree(scores)");
   415	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_distances), "cudaFree(distances)");
   416	    checkCuda(cudaFree(device_points), "cudaFree(points)");
   417	
   418	    return clusters;
   419	}
   420	
   421	// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
   422	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   423	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
   424	                     const double threshold) {
   425	    bool valid = true;
   426	    
   427	    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   428	    
   429	    // Check each cluster
   430	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {

 // QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
 // by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_SEED = 256;
+
+// CUDA errors are fatal.  CUDA is deliberately not optional for this
+// benchmark: silently running a CPU implementation would make its reported
+// performance meaningless.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkKernel(const char* operation) {
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), operation);
+}
+
+// The matrix is laid out as distance[member * N + candidate].  Keeping it
+// resident avoids recalculating the same pairwise distance for every seed and
+// every QT growth iteration.  The 2-D launch gives coalesced writes along the
+// candidate dimension, which is also the access pattern used by score updates.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count || member >= point_count) return;
+
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate] =
+        sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Scores use two negative sentinels.  -1 means that the point is already in
+// this seed's candidate cluster, while -2 means that it was assigned by a
+// previously selected global cluster.  Non-negative values are the current
+// maximum distance from the candidate to this candidate cluster.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateState(const double* distances,
+                                          const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                          double* scores,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (clustered[candidate] != 0) {
+        scores[offset] = -2.0;
+    } else if (seed == candidate) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+    } else {
+        // This is distance(points[candidate], points[seed]), matching the
+        // order used by the original candidate-to-member calculation.
+        scores[offset] = distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeSeedState(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                    unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                    const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const bool is_active = clustered[seed] == 0;
+    active[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+    member_counts[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool isBetter(const double score, const int candidate,
+                                          const double best_score,
+                                          const int best_candidate) {
+    return score < best_score ||
+           (score == best_score && candidate < best_candidate);
+}
+
+// One block owns one seed.  Each thread scans a strided portion of all
+// candidates and the block reduction retains the lowest candidate index on an
+// exact score tie, just like the original ascending CPU loop.
+__global__ void selectClosestCandidates(const double* scores,
+                                        const unsigned char* active,
+                                        int* best_candidates,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+    double best_score = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    if (active[seed] != 0) {
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            const double score = scores[row + candidate];
+            if (score >= 0.0 && score < threshold &&
+                isBetter(score, candidate, best_score, best_candidate)) {
+                best_score = score;
+                best_candidate = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double shared_scores[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    __shared__ int shared_candidates[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = best_score;
+    shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            isBetter(shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_scores[threadIdx.x],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x])) {
+            shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        best_candidates[seed] = shared_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                    ? -1
+                                    : shared_candidates[0];
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the chosen point to every seed's state in parallel.  Once a point is
+// selected, the score update is just one lookup into the resident distance
+// matrix for each still-eligible candidate.
+__global__ void updateCandidateScores(const double* distances,
+                                      const int* best_candidates,
+                                      double* scores, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const int best = best_candidates[seed];
+    if (best < 0) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (candidate == best) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double old_score = scores[offset];
+    if (old_score >= 0.0) {
+        const double new_distance =
+            distances[static_cast<size_t>(best) * point_count + candidate];
+        scores[offset] = old_score > new_distance ? old_score : new_distance;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void commitCandidates(const int* best_candidates,
+                                  unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                  int* added_count, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    if (active[seed] == 0) return;
+
+    if (best_candidates[seed] < 0) {
+        active[seed] = 0;
+    } else {
+        ++member_counts[seed];
+        atomicAdd(added_count, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_SEED = 256;
+
+// CUDA errors are fatal.  CUDA is deliberately not optional for this
+// benchmark: silently running a CPU implementation would make its reported
+// performance meaningless.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkKernel(const char* operation) {
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), operation);
+}
+
+// The matrix is laid out as distance[member * N + candidate].  Keeping it
+// resident avoids recalculating the same pairwise distance for every seed and
+// every QT growth iteration.  The 2-D launch gives coalesced writes along the
+// candidate dimension, which is also the access pattern used by score updates.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count || member >= point_count) return;
+
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate] =
+        sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Scores use two negative sentinels.  -1 means that the point is already in
+// this seed's candidate cluster, while -2 means that it was assigned by a
+// previously selected global cluster.  Non-negative values are the current
+// maximum distance from the candidate to this candidate cluster.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateState(const double* distances,
+                                          const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                          double* scores,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (clustered[candidate] != 0) {
+        scores[offset] = -2.0;
+    } else if (seed == candidate) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+    } else {
+        // This is distance(points[candidate], points[seed]), matching the
+        // order used by the original candidate-to-member calculation.
+        scores[offset] = distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeSeedState(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                    unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                    const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const bool is_active = clustered[seed] == 0;
+    active[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+    member_counts[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool isBetter(const double score, const int candidate,
+                                          const double best_score,
+                                          const int best_candidate) {
+    return score < best_score ||
+           (score == best_score && candidate < best_candidate);
+}
+
+// One block owns one seed.  Each thread scans a strided portion of all
+// candidates and the block reduction retains the lowest candidate index on an
+// exact score tie, just like the original ascending CPU loop.
+__global__ void selectClosestCandidates(const double* scores,
+                                        const unsigned char* active,
+                                        int* best_candidates,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+    double best_score = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    if (active[seed] != 0) {
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            const double score = scores[row + candidate];
+            if (score >= 0.0 && score < threshold &&
+                isBetter(score, candidate, best_score, best_candidate)) {
+                best_score = score;
+                best_candidate = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double shared_scores[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    __shared__ int shared_candidates[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = best_score;
+    shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            isBetter(shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_scores[threadIdx.x],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x])) {
+            shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        best_candidates[seed] = shared_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                    ? -1
+                                    : shared_candidates[0];
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the chosen point to every seed's state in parallel.  Once a point is
+// selected, the score update is just one lookup into the resident distance
+// matrix for each still-eligible candidate.
+__global__ void updateCandidateScores(const double* distances,
+                                      const int* best_candidates,
+                                      double* scores, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const int best = best_candidates[seed];
+    if (best < 0) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (candidate == best) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double old_score = scores[offset];
+    if (old_score >= 0.0) {
+        const double new_distance =
+            distances[static_cast<size_t>(best) * point_count + candidate];
+        scores[offset] = old_score > new_distance ? old_score : new_distance;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void commitCandidates(const int* best_candidates,
+                                  unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                  int* added_count, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    if (active[seed] == 0) return;
+
+    if (best_candidates[seed] < 0) {
+        active[seed] = 0;
+    } else {
+        ++member_counts[seed];
+        atomicAdd(added_count, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

Highlights:

- GPU-resident distance matrix and clustering scores.
- Parallel candidate evaluation and reductions per seed.
- Preserved deterministic tie-breaking and QT semantics.
- CUDA-only execution with `qtclustering` executable unchanged.
- Native `sm_86` build configuration.

Validated successfully with 1,000-, 2,000-, and 5,000-point runs. The 1,000-point result hash is `dbef3881b0a560cd`, and validation passes.

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_SEED = 256;
+
+// CUDA errors are fatal.  CUDA is deliberately not optional for this
+// benchmark: silently running a CPU implementation would make its reported
+// performance meaningless.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkKernel(const char* operation) {
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), operation);
+}
+
+// The matrix is laid out as distance[member * N + candidate].  Keeping it
+// resident avoids recalculating the same pairwise distance for every seed and
+// every QT growth iteration.  The 2-D launch gives coalesced writes along the
+// candidate dimension, which is also the access pattern used by score updates.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate >= point_count || member >= point_count) return;
+
+    const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+    const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+    distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count + candidate] =
+        sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Scores use two negative sentinels.  -1 means that the point is already in
+// this seed's candidate cluster, while -2 means that it was assigned by a
+// previously selected global cluster.  Non-negative values are the current
+// maximum distance from the candidate to this candidate cluster.
+__global__ void initializeCandidateState(const double* distances,
+                                          const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                          double* scores,
+                                          const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (clustered[candidate] != 0) {
+        scores[offset] = -2.0;
+    } else if (seed == candidate) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+    } else {
+        // This is distance(points[candidate], points[seed]), matching the
+        // order used by the original candidate-to-member calculation.
+        scores[offset] = distances[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeSeedState(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                    unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                    const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const bool is_active = clustered[seed] == 0;
+    active[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+    member_counts[seed] = is_active ? 1 : 0;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool isBetter(const double score, const int candidate,
+                                          const double best_score,
+                                          const int best_candidate) {
+    return score < best_score ||
+           (score == best_score && candidate < best_candidate);
+}
+
+// One block owns one seed.  Each thread scans a strided portion of all
+// candidates and the block reduction retains the lowest candidate index on an
+// exact score tie, just like the original ascending CPU loop.
+__global__ void selectClosestCandidates(const double* scores,
+                                        const unsigned char* active,
+                                        int* best_candidates,
+                                        const double threshold,
+                                        const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+    double best_score = DBL_MAX;
+    int best_candidate = point_count;
+
+    if (active[seed] != 0) {
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            const double score = scores[row + candidate];
+            if (score >= 0.0 && score < threshold &&
+                isBetter(score, candidate, best_score, best_candidate)) {
+                best_score = score;
+                best_candidate = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double shared_scores[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    __shared__ int shared_candidates[THREADS_PER_SEED];
+    shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = best_score;
+    shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = best_candidate;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            isBetter(shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride],
+                     shared_scores[threadIdx.x],
+                     shared_candidates[threadIdx.x])) {
+            shared_scores[threadIdx.x] = shared_scores[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            shared_candidates[threadIdx.x] = shared_candidates[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        best_candidates[seed] = shared_candidates[0] == point_count
+                                    ? -1
+                                    : shared_candidates[0];
+    }
+}
+
+// Add the chosen point to every seed's state in parallel.  Once a point is
+// selected, the score update is just one lookup into the resident distance
+// matrix for each still-eligible candidate.
+__global__ void updateCandidateScores(const double* distances,
+                                      const int* best_candidates,
+                                      double* scores, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count || candidate >= point_count) return;
+
+    const int best = best_candidates[seed];
+    if (best < 0) return;
+
+    const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + candidate;
+    if (candidate == best) {
+        scores[offset] = -1.0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const double old_score = scores[offset];
+    if (old_score >= 0.0) {
+        const double new_distance =
+            distances[static_cast<size_t>(best) * point_count + candidate];
+        scores[offset] = old_score > new_distance ? old_score : new_distance;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void commitCandidates(const int* best_candidates,
+                                  unsigned char* active, int* member_counts,
+                                  int* added_count, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    if (active[seed] == 0) return;
+
+    if (best_candidates[seed] < 0) {
+        active[seed] = 0;
+    } else {
+        ++member_counts[seed];
+        atomicAdd(added_count, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
